(1) python3 -I /home/user/Documents/bip_tools/tmp/aud037/verify/AUD-037-UI006/raw.py runs `decode --mode seedshift --input-file r4/public-masked.txt --dates-file r4/public-date.txt`. With colour, the private screen shows "MnemoCode · Recovered result", the facts Mode/Input format/Words, the phrase numbered four to a row, "On one line, for copying:" and "Original fingerprint  73c5da0a  (empty BIP39 passphrase)". With NO_COLOR=1 the same terminal shows only the bare phrase line, "BIP39 checksum: valid." and "Original BIP32 master fingerprint (empty BIP39 passphrase): 73c5da0a". probe.py decode gives the same result for CLICOLOR=0.
(2) python3 -I .../verify/AUD-037-UI006/codes.py runs `encode --mode direct --format 3 --mnemonic-file r4/public-phrase.txt`. With colour it shows a heading and facts, then the codes apart ("7684 7684 ... 5728"), then "Joined, for copying:" and the joined line. With NO_COLOR=1 it shows only "Unicode code points of the original seed phrase itself:" and the 48-digit joined line.
(3) python3 -I .../verify/AUD-037-UI006/probe.py self-test. With colour the heading is "MnemoCode · Self-test 0.1.0" and the summary ends "✓ Passed:". With NO_COLOR=1 or CLICOLOR=0 the heading is "MnemoCode 0.1.0 self-test", there are two box-drawing rule lines, and the summary line is "PASS  ...".
(4) node .../verify/AUD-037-UI006/wrap.mjs, with dist/cli/terminal-style.js and isTTY=true. With colour, a 126-character hint wraps to lines of 77 and 48 characters, a warning starts with "! ", and an error reads "✗ Error: x". With NO_COLOR=1 or CLICOLOR=0, the hint and the warning are each one unwrapped 126-column line with no "!", and the error reads "mnemocode: x".
(5) The reverse case: `CLICOLOR_FORCE=1 node dist/mnemocode.js decode ... | cat`. Stdout is a pipe, yet it gets the full terminal layout (heading, facts, numbered rows). Without the variable it gets the one plain phrase line.
R4's probe_color_vs_nocolor.py is sound apart from a winsize race, because it sets the size after fork. Its output matches mine.
- **Expected behaviour:** On a terminal, NO_COLOR and CLICOLOR=0 should only remove the SGR colour codes. The result layout should stay the same: the "MnemoCode · <what>" heading with label/value facts, running text wrapped at 78 columns (CORE-36), the phrase numbered for writing down, and the "!" mark on warnings. docs/CLI.md:112 also promises that on a terminal, codes written joined (formats 3 and 4) are shown apart and then joined. mhfe is the declared model, and it drops only the colour codes through anstream and keeps the layout. CORE-39 reserves plain sequential output for script mode, redirected output and TERM=dumb.
- **Observed behaviour:** MnemoCode uses one test, streamGetsColor/terminalColor, to decide both colour and layout. With NO_COLOR=1 or CLICOLOR=0 on a real terminal, results switch to the plain script form. Decode loses its heading, facts and numbered phrase. Encode in format 3 shows only the joined code line, which contradicts docs/CLI.md:112. The self-test prints its pipe layout, with box rules and "PASS". Hints and notices are no longer wrapped at 78 columns, warnings lose their "!" mark, and errors read "mnemocode: ..." instead of "✗ Error: ...". The same coupling works in reverse: CLICOLOR_FORCE=1 gives the terminal layout to a pipe. The claim's statements and locations are accurate. However, CORE-38 alone, which the proposal cites, only requires colour to be turned off. The violated contracts are docs/CLI.md:112, CORE-36, CORE-39 and the mhfe model.
- **Impact:** People who set NO_COLOR, often for accessibility or readability, get a different and less helpful layout on the private screen. The phrase is not numbered for writing down, and Unicode codes are not shown apart to help a careful transcription. Long hints and warnings are not wrapped, and warnings have no visible mark. All the data is still present and correct, so there is no secret, integrity or recovery loss. The reverse coupling means a script environment with CLICOLOR_FORCE=1 receives the decorated terminal layout instead of the plain form.
- **Evidence:** Proposed by reviewer R4; verified adversarially at f8d9a93 (confirmed-with-changes); the verifiers' full reasoning is kept in the local evidence (verification/workflow-result.json).
- **Recommended fix:** Split the two decisions in terminal-style.ts and terminal.ts. Use one predicate for the human terminal layout, for example the stream is a TTY that is not TERM=dumb (streamCanRedraw), and another for colour (streamGetsColor). The result header, numbered phrase, apart codes, self-test layout, hint and notice wrapping, warning mark and error prefix should follow the layout predicate. paint() alone should follow NO_COLOR, CLICOLOR=0 and CLICOLOR_FORCE. Then a NO_COLOR terminal keeps the layout without SGR codes, and CLICOLOR_FORCE adds colour without changing the plain form of a pipe, unless the owner decides otherwise and documents it. Update the doc comments that say "returns false without colour". The tests that use NO_COLOR as a stand-in for "not a terminal" will need to stub isTTY instead.
- **Required verification:** PTY tests at 80 columns, with TERM=xterm and NO_COLOR=1, and again with CLICOLOR=0. Decode should show "MnemoCode · Recovered result", the facts and the numbered rows. Encode in format 3 should show the codes apart and then joined. Self-test should show "MnemoCode · Self-test", with no box rules and no "PASS". A hint longer than 78 columns should wrap, a warning should keep "!", and the captured output should contain no SGR sequence. A pipe test with CLICOLOR_FORCE=1 should check that the decode stdout stays the plain one-line form. The existing colour and plain-pipe tests should pass unchanged.

##### AUD-037-UI007 — Low — The table lookup prints its facts as plain, unindented lowercase lines under a coloured heading instead of grey labels

- **Category:** UI
- **Severity:** low
- **Status:** open
- **Release blocking:** false: The defect is only in how the lookup looks on a colour terminal. The word, index and Unicode values are correct, the plain script output is correct, and no security, integrity, recovery, legal or explicit release-precondition guarantee is affected.
- **Rules:** CORE-36
- **Related:** AUD-033-UI001
- **Affected files and builds:** src/cli/table-command.ts:24-29 (printRow). Contract: src/cli/terminal.ts:89-104 (terminalResultHeader doc comment) and src/cli/terminal-style.ts:106-125 (factLine/resultLines). Tests that pin the current form: test/scan-fixes-s006-s061.test.ts:150 and scripts/terminal_menu_checks.py:145-146.
- **Reproduction:** Ran /home/user/Documents/bip_tools/tmp/aud037/verify/AUD-037-UI007/probe.sh, which runs the built dist/mnemocode.js. `CLICOLOR_FORCE=1 node dist/mnemocode.js table --word abandon | cat -v` printed a blank line, then `^[[1;36mMnemoCode^[[0m ^[[90mM-BM-7^[[0m ^[[1mWord mapping^[[0m`, then the uncoloured, unindented lines `index:       1`, `english:     abandon` and `unicode hex: 7684`, with exit 0. `table --index 1` printed the same layout. With NO_COLOR=1 the same three plain lines appeared without the heading. In the source, printRow at HEAD calls `terminalResultHeader("Word mapping", [])` with no rows and then always prints the three labels with console.log. The file is byte-identical at f8d9a93 and at the checkout HEAD 7567ab5, whose two extra commits only touch docs, package.json and vitest.config.ts. The menu's word lookup (src/cli/menu.ts:563) runs the same `table --<lookup>` command. The PTY check scripts/terminal_menu_checks.py:145-146 waits for `english:     about` and `unicode hex: 5728` in a colour session, so the interactive menu shows the same layout.
- **Expected behaviour:** CORE-36 says a result starts with "<Tool> · <what>" and lists its facts as grey labels with their values. The doc comment of terminalResultHeader repeats this. With colour, the lookup's facts should therefore come out through resultLines/factLine: indented, with grey, aligned labels written like every other result's labels (for example "Installed" or "Content"). The plain form should be kept only when there is no colour.
- **Observed behaviour:** With colour, the lookup prints the styled "MnemoCode · Word mapping" heading. Its three facts follow as unindented, uncoloured, lowercase script lines ("index:", "english:", "unicode hex:"), because printRow passes an empty row list to terminalResultHeader and ignores its return value. Every other result header passes its facts as rows or prints its plain form only when there is no colour. This lookup is the only one that mixes the two.
- **Impact:** The single-word lookup looks inconsistent on a colour terminal, both from the command line and from the interactive menu, and breaks the workspace result-layout rule. The values are correct and the plain script output is unaffected.
- **Evidence:** Proposed by reviewer R4; verified adversarially at f8d9a93 (confirmed); the verifiers' full reasoning is kept in the local evidence (verification/workflow-result.json).
- **Recommended fix:** In printRow, pass the three facts as rows to terminalResultHeader. Print the existing plain lines (index:/english:/unicode hex:) only when it returns false, as encode-report.ts and sskr-export-command.ts already do. Keep the plain lines byte-for-byte for pipes and scripts. Update scripts/terminal_menu_checks.py to expect the grey-label form in the colour PTY session. Add a colour-forced test next to the existing plain-output test.
- **Required verification:** `CLICOLOR_FORCE=1 node dist/mnemocode.js table --word abandon | cat -v` should show three indented facts, each with a `^[[90m` grey label, under the heading and without the lowercase script lines. `NO_COLOR=1` and piped output should stay exactly `index:       1`, `english:     abandon`, `unicode hex: 7684`. test/scan-fixes-s006-s061.test.ts, a new colour-forced test and the updated terminal_menu_checks.py menu lookup should all pass.

##### AUD-037-UI008 — Low — Help group headings exceed the 80-column help width; help cannot show version and help

- **Category:** UI
- **Severity:** low
- **Status:** open
- **Release blocking:** false: Presentation-only: one help heading line wraps in an 80-column terminal, and the help of the two built-in commands is refused with a clear message. No security, integrity or recovery guarantee, legal obligation, documented safety check or release-precondition rule is affected.
- **Rules:** CORE-37, DOC-06
- **Related:** AUD-005-UI002
- **Affected files and builds:** src/cli/help-pages.ts:227-228 (decode group heading, 87 columns); src/cli/help-pages.ts:319-320 (re-encode group heading, 83 columns); src/cli/usage.ts:21-22 (HELP_WIDTH contract); src/cli/usage.ts:149-151 (section() prints headings unwrapped); src/cli/usage.ts:197-200 and 235 (version and help listed as commands; "-h, --help: Print help; after a command, its help"); src/cli/usage.ts:302-312 (printNamedHelp knows only commandHelp and topicHelp); src/cli/command-line.ts:87-98 (help and version dispatch); test/help.test.ts:54-55 (width check covers only the overview); README.md:40 ("For options, run mnemocode <command> --help")
- **Reproduction:** Source is identical between f8d9a93 and the working HEAD 61e163b: git diff f8d9a93 61e163b changes only docs, package.json and vitest.config.ts. dist/ matches it. Probe: sh /home/user/Documents/bip_tools/tmp/aud037/verify/AUD-037-UI008/probe.sh, output in /home/user/Documents/bip_tools/tmp/aud037/verify/AUD-037-UI008/probe.log, run with NO_COLOR=1 node dist/mnemocode.js. Results: "help decode" line 165 and "decode -h" line 38 are 87 columns ("Wallet check, for dates or codes with ?, and for the shares when a share is typed here:"); "help re-encode" line 127 and "re-encode -h" line 28 are 83 columns ("The new backup (without these, --ask-secrets asks it once the phrase is confirmed):"). With CLICOLOR_FORCE=1 the same heading is a single bold-cyan line with no wrap. No other line in the overview or in any command or topic page goes past 80 columns, except the TEST_PHRASE='abandon ... about' shell line (109 columns), which cannot be split and is outside this candidate. "help version" and "help help" exit 1 with "There is no such command or topic. Choose one of: encode, ..., cards." "version --help" and "version -h" exit 1 with "The version command does not accept additional arguments." "help --help" and "help -h" are refused the same way. The overview lists "version  Print the version" and "help  Print this help, or that of a command" under Commands, and "-h, --help  Print help; after a command, its help" under Options. git log -S shows the two long headings were added in be33cc4 (2026-10-07) and 8aa9b25 (2026-10-14), after AUD-005-UI002 was verified on 2026-10-01.
- **Expected behaviour:** Every help line fits the 80-column help width, as stated by the HELP_WIDTH comment ("an 80-column terminal, also in a wider one"). AUD-005-UI002 also records it as expected behaviour ("every other help text here wraps at 80 columns"). The one exception is the copyable TEST_PHRASE shell assignment. Commands listed under "Commands:" either answer "--help" after them and "help <command>", as the overview's Options text and README promise, or are not presented as commands.
- **Observed behaviour:** section() prints group headings without wrapping. The decode help (both -h and --help/help decode) has an 87-column heading and the re-encode help has an 83-column heading. "version" and "help" are listed as commands, yet "help version", "help help", "version --help" and "version -h" all exit 1 with an error. The error's list of valid names leaves out version and help.
- **Impact:** In an 80-column terminal the two option-group headings wrap mid-phrase, breaking the clap-style layout that the help imitates. A user who follows the overview's "after a command, its help", or the README's "run mnemocode <command> --help", gets an error for the version command. Presentation and consistency only. No secret, record or recovery result is affected.
- **Evidence:** Proposed by reviewer R4+R7; verified adversarially at f8d9a93 (confirmed-with-changes); the verifiers' full reasoning is kept in the local evidence (verification/workflow-result.json).
- **Recommended fix:** Shorten the two group headings, or wrap headings at HELP_WIDTH in section(). Extend the AUD-005-UI002 width check in test/help.test.ts from the overview to every command page (-h and --help) and every topic page, exempting only the TEST_PHRASE line. For version and help, choose one contract and make the help match it. Either accept "help version", "help help" and "version -h/--help" (print a short page or the overview, as clap does for its built-in subcommands), or move version and help out of the "Commands:" list and reword "after a command, its help".
- **Required verification:** A help test runs every command with -h and --help, "help <command>" and "help <topic>" under NO_COLOR=1 and asserts that no line except the TEST_PHRASE assignment exceeds 80 columns. It must fail at f8d9a93 on decode and re-encode and pass after the fix. CLI tests cover "help version", "help help", "version --help" and "version -h" with the chosen behaviour, and the overview and README wording is checked against it.

##### AUD-037-ARC002 — Low — Rules, limits, conversions and messages written twice where the lexical copies gate cannot see them

- **Category:** ARC
- **Severity:** low
- **Status:** open
- **Release blocking:** false: Every duplicated copy agrees with its counterpart at f8d9a93. No output, refusal or secret-handling difference was shown. - **Rules:** CORE-10, CORE-01
- **Related:** AUD-011-ARC077, AUD-011-ARC071, AUD-021-ARC001
- **Affected files and builds:** src/export/color-math.ts:24,71; src/export/card-palette.ts:31,43,64; src/export/business-tint.ts:31,56; src/core/candidate-list.ts:157; src/core/master-fingerprint.ts:87; src/export/card-settings.ts:123-126; src/export/heir-sheet.ts:94-97; src/cli/decode-command.ts:244-245; src/cli/re-encode-command.ts:180-184; src/cli/share-repair.ts:72,327 vs src/sskr/share-input.ts:58,467; src/cli/help-pages.ts:651 vs src/cli/menu-entries.ts:23; src/core/refusal.ts:22 vs src/cli/output-paths.ts:171,270, src/export/private-file.ts:119,137, src/export/image-export.ts:163; src/core/candidate-list.ts:31-32; src/sskr/bridge.ts:60-63; src/cli/input-bytes.ts:6; src/core/word-table.ts:49 vs src/core/words.ts:121 (and src/core/typed-number.ts:13); src/cli/encode-export.ts:27-31,84-103 vs src/export/sskr-render.ts:107-122
- **Reproduction:** The checkout's HEAD is 61e163b. `git diff --stat f8d9a93 61e163b` touches only docs, package.json and vitest.config.ts, so src/ matches f8d9a93. Probes run:
(1) Reviewer probe (sound: it reads the src/ tree): `node tmp/aud037/r6/dups.mjs <repo> regex` reports `/\p{Cs}/u` at candidate-list.ts:157 and master-fingerprint.ts:87, and `/^\d+$/u` at 5 sites, including word-table.ts:49 and words.ts:121. `... strings` reports `${width} x ${height} mm` at card-settings.ts:125 and heir-sheet.ts:96, the checksum template at decode-command.ts:244 and re-encode-command.ts:182, and `No share was typed at place ${value0}.` at share-repair.ts:72 and share-input.ts:58.
(2) Own probe `sh /home/user/Documents/bip_tools/tmp/aud037/verify/AUD-037-ARC002/sites.sh f8d9a93` (git grep at f8d9a93). It shows `BYTE_MAX = 255` (color-math.ts:24) and `CHANNEL_MAX = 255` twice (card-palette.ts:31, business-tint.ts:31), each dividing bytes by 255. It also shows errno codes read three ways: errorCode() in 4 places, an unchecked `(error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code` in 4 places, and an inline `"code" in error` once. Limits are written as literals in messages ("16 to 32 bytes", "from 0 to 255" twice), and the plannedCardJob/separateCardJob caching sits in cli/encode-export.ts.
(3) `node /home/user/Documents/bip_tools/tmp/aud037/verify/AUD-037-ARC002/short-strings.mjs <repo> f8d9a93` shows "Run the self-test" at help-pages.ts:651 and menu-entries.ts:23. It also shows "Record/QR code/Text file name" in menu.ts and encode-questions.ts.
(4) `git log --diff-filter=A` dates card-palette.ts and business-tint.ts to 5cfb026 (2026-10-13). That is after AUD-012 recorded AUD-011-ARC077 as source-fixed.
(5) The browser host's own plan cache is MnemoCodeCardPlans in multi-chain-wallet-tools packages/recovery-backup/src/mnemocode-card-plans.ts, which was read but is outside this repository.
The header of scripts/verify-no-copies.mjs says it does not find "the same rule written in other words ... nor a constant written in many places".
- **Expected behaviour:** CORE-10 says every rule, limit, constant, conversion, message and question has exactly one source, and that a value derived from another is derived in code. CORE-01 says a rule lives in a library module, and front ends only translate input and output. menu-entries.ts states that every text naming a menu entry takes the name from MENU_ENTRY_NAMES.
- **Observed behaviour:** Several rules have second copies that the lexical copies gate cannot see.
- The 255 byte maximum and the byte-to-channel division are written in color-math.ts, card-palette.ts and business-tint.ts. This regresses AUD-011-ARC077, which AUD-012 recorded as having one owner.
- The lone-surrogate test `/\p{Cs}/u` is written twice, with two different refusal codes.
- The "W x H mm" label is formatted in both pageSizeLabel and heirSheetSizeLabel.
- The plain "BIP39 checksum: …" line and its follow-up fingerprint report are written in both decode-command.ts and re-encode-command.ts.
- share-repair.ts repeats ShareInput.replace's "No share was typed at place N" precondition with its own code.
- help-pages.ts writes the menu label "Run the self-test" literally.
- errno codes are read three ways, although errorCode() exists.
- candidate-list.ts, bridge.ts and input-bytes.ts write limits as literals in messages, although BIP39_ENTROPY_BYTES and U8_MAX exist.
- words.ts redefines WORD_NUMBER (`/^\d+$/u`) and passes it to WordTable.indexOfNumber, whose default is the same regex.
- The backup cards' plan-once, reuse and separate-card conversion is kept in the CLI (EncodeCardExport), while shares have a library owner (ShareCardSet). The CardValues split and recompose code is written in both encode-export.ts and sskr-render.ts.
Two parts of the claim do not hold as stated:
- The file-name labels in menu.ts and encode-questions.ts belong to different questions (input vs output, different defaults and checks), so they are not one question.
- No browser host exists in this repository. The host's second plan cache is in multi-chain-wallet-tools.
- **Impact:** The impact is maintainability only. Every copy agrees today, so no wrong output, refusal or secret exposure was shown. A future change to one copy can make labels, refusal codes, limits or colour conversion drift apart between commands and between the CLI and the browser host. Hosts that match on refusal codes already see two different codes for the same lone-surrogate condition.
- **Evidence:** Proposed by reviewer R6+R5; verified adversarially at f8d9a93 (confirmed-with-changes); the verifiers' full reasoning is kept in the local evidence (verification/workflow-result.json).
- **Recommended fix:** - Export one byte-to-channel helper (and BYTE_MAX) from color-math.ts. Use rgbChannels in business-tint.ts and hexOfBytes or that helper in card-palette.ts.
- Add one well-formed-text predicate in core and call it from both candidate-list.ts and master-fingerprint.ts, keeping their messages if the @scure/bip39 wording is intended.
- Add one millimetre-size formatter that both size labels use.
- Move the plain checksum and fingerprint line into fingerprint-report.ts and call it from decode and re-encode.
- Let share-repair rely on ShareInput's refusal (or a shared rule) instead of its own code.
- Take the help summary from MENU_ENTRY_NAMES.selfTest.label.
- Replace the NodeJS.ErrnoException casts (also a TS-04 deviation) and the inline check with errorCode().
- Build the limit messages from BIP39_ENTROPY_BYTES and U8_MAX.
- Drop words.ts's WORD_NUMBER in favour of WordTable's default, or one exported constant.
- Give backup cards a library plan owner like ShareCardSet, which both the CLI and the browser host can use.
- **Required verification:** Rerun sites.sh and dups.mjs and check that each item has one definition left. Existing message, code and output tests must pass unchanged, including the card-palette golden colours, the business artwork goldens and the heir-sheet and card size labels. Add tests that bind the limit messages to BIP39_ENTROPY_BYTES and U8_MAX, and the help summary to MENU_ENTRY_NAMES. Then pass pnpm check (including copies and code-rules), the full tests, the self-test and the PTY checks.

##### AUD-037-ARC003 — Low — Explanatory comments in joint-solve.ts were shortened under a line gate that counts comments, and type-only import cycles escape the TS-15 gate

- **Category:** ARC
- **Severity:** low
- **Status:** open
- **Release blocking:** false: This is a maintainability and gate-coverage defect. No security, integrity or recovery guarantee is affected, there are no value import cycles, so module load order is unaffected, and neither CORE-15 nor TS-15 is stated as a precondition of a release.
- **Rules:** CORE-15, CORE-19, CORE-51, TS-15
- **Related:** AUD-035 CHECK-ARC-002, AUD-025-ARC001, AUD-037-BLD006, AUD-037-DOC019
- **Affected files and builds:** src/sskr/joint-solve.ts:1-3,138-139,267-271,338-339,402-406,774 (comments shortened in d4776f7); .oxlintrc.json:9-24 (max-lines and max-lines-per-function with skipComments:false and skipBlankLines:false); scripts/verify-code-rules.mjs:38-39,148-174 (counts physical lines); .oxlintrc.json:25 (import/no-cycle without ignoreTypes:false); src/core/wallet-evidence-owner.ts:8 and src/core/wallet-evidence.ts:70; src/sskr/set-topology.ts:9 and src/sskr/transport.ts:41; src/export/business-cards.ts:15, src/export/material-cards.ts:22 and src/export/templates.ts:1,13; src/cli/encode-export.ts:17, src/cli/encode-report.ts:15, src/cli/sskr-split-command.ts:24,46, src/cli/encode-command.ts:9-12 and src/cli/sskr-command.ts:14; minor: src/sskr/repair.ts:17-22, whose header describes MarkedReading and ReadingSpace without naming repair-owners.ts, where they now live
- **Reproduction:** 1) git diff 44f7c73 d4776f7 -- src/sskr/joint-solve.ts, saved as /home/user/Documents/bip_tools/tmp/aud037/verify/AUD-037-ARC003/joint-solve-44f7c73-d4776f7.diff. The diff has 35 removed and 19 added comment lines. The multi-line docs of Branch, searchDigest, #confirm, AssessmentSecrets and restore became one or two lines. The removed text includes "in Gray code order so that each step adds one known change to (S, D)". 2) node /home/user/Documents/bip_tools/tmp/aud037/verify/AUD-037-ARC003/loc.mjs <rev> src/sskr/joint-solve.ts gives: 44f7c73 physical=685 comment-only=140 code=491; d4776f7 physical=794 comment-only=124 code=611; f8d9a93 physical=800 comment-only=125 code=616. For wallet-evidence.ts at f8d9a93 it gives physical=794 code=513. 3) git show f8d9a93:.oxlintrc.json shows max-lines {max:800, skipBlankLines:false, skipComments:false}, max-lines-per-function the same, and "import/no-cycle": "error" with no options. 4) node /home/user/Documents/bip_tools/tmp/aud037/verify/AUD-037-ARC003/cycle-edges.mjs f8d9a93 gives "cycles counting type-only imports: 4; cycles of value imports only: 0" and lists each closing edge as [type-only], for example wallet-evidence-owner.ts:8 -> wallet-evidence.ts. The same probe finds 4 cycles at 261aaed (the AUD-035 commit) and 3 at 870effe and 44f7c73. 5) A six-file fixture in /home/user/Documents/bip_tools/tmp/aud037/verify/AUD-037-ARC003/cyc was run with the repository's oxlint 1.88.0 (oxlint --no-ignore -c oxlintrc.json a.ts ... f.ts). Pair a/b uses import type, pair e/f uses an inline { type B } import, and pair c/d uses value imports. With the repository's rule form only c.ts and d.ts were reported. With {"ignoreTypes": false} all six files were reported. 6) Line counts of wallet-evidence.ts in every commit since f2d4cff are 793, 797 and 794. Its 26-line catalog was moved out in f2d4cff; inline it would put the file at about 819 lines. 7) Searched docs/REMEDIATION.md and AUD-001 to AUD-036. No tracked item covers comment trimming, the max-lines counting mode or type-only cycles. AUD-035 CHECK-ARC-002 reported "no import cycles" at 261aaed, where these 4 type-only cycles already existed.
- **Expected behaviour:** Under CORE-15 only lines of code count against the 800 and 150 limits, and a comment that explains is never removed or shortened to fit. The lint gate should configure max-lines and max-lines-per-function with skipComments:true and skipBlankLines:true, and the code-rules gate should count lines of code. Under TS-15 modules import each other without cycles, and the lint gate's import/no-cycle should report every cycle, including those closed by type-only imports.
- **Observed behaviour:** At f8d9a93 both gates count physical lines, comments and blank lines included. joint-solve.ts sits at exactly 800 lines after d4776f7 shortened still-valid explanatory doc comments while adding code; the file has only 616 lines of code. Four import cycles closed by type-only imports exist in src. oxlint's import/no-cycle, configured with its default, ignores type-only imports, so pnpm check passes. In the same commit that added the wallet-evidence-owner.ts cycle, the project created the repair-types.ts leaf module to avoid a cycle of the same kind.
- **Impact:** Readers of the joint SSKR repair lose the stated reasons for its search order and for its secret-ownership classes. The gate keeps pushing authors to trade comments for code near the limit, against CORE-15 and CORE-19. Type-only cycles couple modules that TS-15 says should be one module or should share a leaf module, and the gate and AUD-035's check report them as absent. There is no runtime, security or recovery effect.
- **Evidence:** Proposed by reviewer R9+R5; verified adversarially at f8d9a93 (confirmed-with-changes); the verifiers' full reasoning is kept in the local evidence (verification/workflow-result.json).
- **Recommended fix:** Set skipComments:true and skipBlankLines:true on max-lines and max-lines-per-function in .oxlintrc.json. Make verify-code-rules.mjs count lines of code by each language's comment syntax. Restore the removed rationale in joint-solve.ts, adjusted to the new borrow/own model: the Gray-code reason, what a Branch holds, when AssessmentSecrets wipes, and the restore rules. Configure import/no-cycle with {"ignoreTypes": false}. Break the four cycles by moving the shared types into leaf modules, the way repair-types.ts does. For example, move EvidenceMatch out of wallet-evidence.ts and the transport types used by set-topology.ts into such modules. Optionally update the header of repair.ts to name repair-owners.ts.
- **Required verification:** pnpm lint passes with the new counting options and with import/no-cycle {"ignoreTypes": false}. Before the cycles are broken, a fixture or temporary run shows the rule reporting the four type-only cycles. Afterwards the cycle probe reports 0 cycles counting type-only edges. A diff of joint-solve.ts shows the rationale restored. A code-rules test proves that comment and blank lines are not counted, and that a file over 800 lines of code is still refused.

##### AUD-037-ARC004 — Low — A refusal test cannot fail for the rule it names, and type assertions lack the comment TS-04 requires

- **Category:** ARC
- **Severity:** low
- **Status:** open
- **Release blocking:** false: CORE-28 and TS-04 are review-checked coding rules, not stated release preconditions. Each refusal cited is also pinned exactly by another test, so no security, integrity or recovery check goes untested.
- **Rules:** CORE-28, TS-04
- **Affected files and builds:** test/sskr-vectors.test.ts:97-100; test/known-audit-defects.test.ts:75-89; src/cli/bitcoin-options.ts:206,215 (with src/core/wallet-evidence.ts:420); src/export/card-drawing.ts:120-123,239
- **Reproduction:** Working-tree src/ and test/ are identical to f8d9a93 (git diff --quiet f8d9a93 -- test src; the later commit 61e163b changes only docs, package.json and vitest.config.ts).
(1) node tmp/aud037/verify/AUD-037-ARC004/broad.mjs <repo> list printed "matches 126; .not.toThrow() 48; positive broad 78 in 35 files; error pinned within 12 lines after 24".
(2) Counting broad matches in each tree gave 118 at 261aaed (the AUD-032/034/035/036 base) and 126 at f8d9a93. git diff 261aaed f8d9a93 -- test adds 8 such lines, only 2 of them positive (self-test-integrity.test.ts:478 and aud-033-working-memory.test.ts:107). Only 4 test files with a broad match are new, and 1 of them has a positive one.
(3) node tmp/aud037/verify/AUD-037-ARC004/refusals.mjs <repo> calls the dist modules with the vector's first share (last color #670000):
- "#420001" (the test input) gives mixed-share-refused.
- "#420000" (the same data change with zero padding, which is what the test input meets when the padding rule is gone, because mixedTransport drops the padding bytes) gives sskr-transport-checksum-does-not-match.
- "#670001" (padding only) gives mixed-share-refused.
- parseInput and formatEncoded with "misspelled" give core.encoded-form.unsupported-encoded-format.
- A missing entry read as EncodedForm.#values["misspelled"].read would be when the guard at src/core/encoded-form.ts:213 is gone gives a TypeError.
(4) node tmp/aud037/r6/as-casts.mjs <repo> list printed "as-assertions 87, with a comment nearby 28". It counts three `as unknown as` twice (qr-input.ts:31, candidate-list-self-test.ts:103, card-drawing.ts:239), which leaves 84 distinct assertions, 57 of them without a comment in the two lines above.
(5) Other tests pin the same rules exactly: test/sskr-repair-engine.test.ts:192-199 (padding only, via filled[last] |= 1, expecting undefined), test/aud-026-core.test.ts:46 and test/aud-009-remediation.test.ts:241 (the unknown-format code).
- **Expected behaviour:** CORE-28 says a check must be able to fail: a test named for a refusal should fail when that refusal is removed, so its input should break only that rule or the test should assert the error code. TS-04 says a type assertion other than `as const` sits after a shape check and has a comment that names the check.
- **Observed behaviour:** 48 of the 126 matches are `.not.toThrow()` success assertions, so there are 78 positive broad refusal assertions in 35 files, not 126 in 51. 24 of the 78 are followed by an assertion that pins the error, and some are absence or cause checks rather than input refusals. Only 2 were added since the last audit base, not 18.
A concrete CORE-28 case: test/sskr-vectors.test.ts:99 is named for nonzero padding but its input also changes a data byte. Without the padding rule the CRC still refuses it, so the bare toThrow() cannot fail. test/known-audit-defects.test.ts:76,82 would also pass on a TypeError if the EncodedForm.of guard were removed.
TS-04: src/cli/bitcoin-options.ts:206,215 cast the evidence object to WalletEvidence with no comment naming the check; isPlacedKind returns boolean, not a type predicate. src/export/card-drawing.ts:239 does check nothing, but the reason for the cast is commented on OutlinePath at line 120, so the claim that it is uncommented is wrong.
- **Impact:** Maintainability only. Some refusal tests would stay green if the rule they name regressed. The cited rules are still covered by exact assertions elsewhere, so no regression is shown to escape the suite. Uncommented casts hide from the type checker what was proven about the value.
- **Evidence:** Proposed by reviewer R6; verified adversarially at f8d9a93 (confirmed-with-changes); the verifiers' full reasoning is kept in the local evidence (verification/workflow-result.json).
- **Recommended fix:** - In test/sskr-vectors.test.ts:99, change only the padding byte (for example "#670001") and assert code sskr.transport.mixed-share-refused.
- Make the known-audit-defects AUD-001-API001 cases assert their codes, and do the same for the other positive bare toThrow()/rejects.toThrow() refusals that the next lines do not pin.
- Make isPlacedKind a type predicate so the WalletEvidence casts become unnecessary, or add a comment naming the check.
- Add a comment naming the check, or a check, to the remaining uncommented `as T` assertions in src.
- **Required verification:** - Rerun broad.mjs and as-casts.mjs. Each remaining positive broad assertion should be either pinned or justified, and each `as T` in src should be `as const` or carry a comment naming its check.
- Show that sskr-vectors.test.ts fails when the padding rule in src/sskr/mixed-layout.ts:226 is disabled in a scratch copy.
- Run pnpm check and the affected test files.

### Informational observations and recommendations

- Refuted as findings, kept as observations: exported tables, the render-asset allowlist and nodeRenderPlatform can be changed at run time (no contract broken; optional hardening); npm pack includes untracked files and 32.6 MB of audit records (the package is not published to npm; audit records are packed by design); the Encode menu walk test timed out at 4978a62 under load and was split at f8d9a93.
- Design quality of the rework (R9, owner's question): not optimal in principle. src/ grew 13% (50,924 to 57,668 lines) and tests 27% for modest new behaviour; about 3,000 added lines are three mechanisms applied everywhere: error catalogs (1,333), secret lifetimes (1,092) and cancellation plumbing (619). Cancellation is close to minimal; nothing material got slower. The verifiers judged ErrorCatalog, SecretValue and the compatibility layers as design judgement calls under CORE-51/52 rather than defects; the owner has asked for them to be simplified in remediation.
- Error codes: 363 of 420 codes with a literal message are word slugs of that message (padm-length-2-more, use-four-digit-year-example-23-09-2026), 143 message functions take value0 parameters, three namespaces are shared by two modules. They are stable literals, so the verifiers kept only the conflation as a finding (AUD-037-API009); redesigning them by condition before 0.1.0 avoids freezing them as the host contract.
- Audit records AUD-030 to AUD-032 embed per-file ledgers (AUD-031 JSON 13.5 MB, 93% ledgers); the owner has asked to keep only the report pair and harnesses published and to remove the ledgers from history in the pre-release regroup.
- CORE-27 ordering (not reproduced, needs engine fault injection): the SSKR known answers run only inside the share commands' prepare step, after a share has been typed into decode or the old backup has been confirmed in re-encode.
- Performance (predates the rework): the date decode builds the @scure/bip39 coder on every call; using the existing ENGLISH_INDEX and entropyOfWords fast paths would make MnemoCode Seedshift date searches markedly faster.
- Process: the concurrent agent's 61e163b fixed three candidates read from the coordinator's unpublished working list and pushed them as a new commit; corrections of unreleased work should be fixup commits of the commit they correct (AGENTS.md Commits). 100 local refs/copilot/checkpoints commits are unsigned and dated +0300; they must never be pushed.

### Assessment and limitations

The October rework left every automatic gate green and the core encodings, derivations and SSKR arithmetic correct against independent oracles. The defects are elsewhere: in behaviour the tests do not look at (what a person can read on the private screen, what happens on Ctrl+C during an export, an ambiguous search reported as one result, a search that silently assumes one colour offset), in secret residue the owner types do not reach (the WASM stack and heap), in rules checked by review rather than gates (error code design, duplicates, comments, doc comments), in documents that promise more than the code does, and in the release process (an unregrouped history and missing MIT notices).

Release acceptance: FAIL. Three findings block the release (AUD-037-UI001, AUD-037-BLD001, AUD-037-BLD002); the seven medium findings should be fixed before tagging. This report is a record of what was examined at f8d9a93, not a certification.

- Windows and macOS were not run locally; their results come from GitHub CI runs read with gh, and AUD-037-SEC001 is established from libuv behaviour and a Linux mechanism probe, not on Windows.
- Browser acceptance in multi-chain-wallet-tools was not executed; e28d81e (after the snapshot) records it as completed, which this audit did not verify.
- The Docker toolchain and dependency stages of the SSKR build were cached (pinned by digest and checksum); only the network-free compile stage was rebuilt without cache.
- No fuzzing, constant-time study, assembly-level zeroization proof or physical print test; WASM memory residue was measured by memory scans only.
- The review was interrupted once at the owner's request and resumed on the new snapshot; checks are recorded per snapshot (4978a62 and f8- for f8d9a93), never added together.
- Local evidence (logs, command records, snapshots, probes) stays in docs/audits/AUD-037-evidence/ and the workspace tmp/aud037/ folder and is not published.
